        (i.e. prev_obs, prev_act(prev_obs), obs(prev_act))
    """
    prev_act_mod = prev_act
    obs_mod = (obs[0], obs[1], obs[2], (obs[3][-1] * 256.0).astype(np.uint8))
    rew_mod = rew
    terminated_mod = terminated
    truncated_mod = truncated
    info_mod = info
    return prev_act_mod, obs_mod, rew_mod, terminated_mod, truncated_mod, info_mod


# FUNCTIONS ====================================================


def last_true_in_list(li):
    for i in reversed(range(len(li))):
        if li[i]:
            return i
    return None


def replace_hist_before_eoe(hist, eoe_idx_in_hist):
    """
    Pads the history hist before the End Of Episode (EOE) index.

    Previous entries in hist are padded with copies of the first element occurring after EOE.
    """
    last_idx = len(hist) - 1
    assert eoe_idx_in_hist <= last_idx, (
        f"replace_hist_before_eoe: eoe_idx_in_hist:{eoe_idx_in_hist}, last_idx:{last_idx}"
    )
    if 0 <= eoe_idx_in_hist < last_idx:
        for i in reversed(range(len(hist))):
            if i <= eoe_idx_in_hist:
                hist[i] = hist[i + 1]


# ============================= HORIZONTAL FLIP AUGMENTATION ================================


def _hflip_tqcgrab_obs(obs_tuple, n_obs, points_number, has_curvature=False, has_images=False):
    """Mirror a TQCGrab-structured observation horizontally.

    Applies to the 14-component base obs (TQCGRABObsIndex 0-13), optional curvature (idx 14),
    optional images (last obs slot), and historical action buffer (indices >= n_obs).
    Track: negate lateral dx and swap left/right edges.
    Physics: negate input_steer, aim_yaw, steer_angle (+ swap FL/FR), swap slip_coef FL/FR.

    Compatibility: Only valid when obs[0] is the track array (layout left/center/right, 6*N).
    Use with: MemoryR2D2woImages, MemoryR2D2Sophy, MemoryR2D2TQCWithImages.
    Do NOT use with MemoryTMLidar or MemoryTMFull (obs[0] is speed there); would crash.
    """
    obs = list(obs_tuple)
    n = points_number

    track = np.array(obs[0], dtype=np.float32, copy=True)
    # Critical: with TRACK_LOCAL_FRAME, (dx, dz) are car-relative. Negate all X (dx) first,
    # then swap left/right boundaries so the flipped track is geometrically consistent.
    track[0::2] *= -1.0
    left = track[0 : 2 * n].copy()
    right = track[4 * n : 6 * n].copy()
    track[0 : 2 * n] = right
    track[4 * n : 6 * n] = left
    obs[0] = track

    obs[5] = -np.asarray(obs[5], dtype=np.float32)

    obs[9] = -np.asarray(obs[9], dtype=np.float32)

    sa = np.asarray(obs[11], dtype=np.float32)
    obs[11] = np.array([-sa[1], -sa[0]], dtype=np.float32)

    sc = np.asarray(obs[12], dtype=np.float32)
    obs[12] = np.array([sc[1], sc[0]], dtype=np.float32)

    if has_curvature:
        obs[14] = -np.asarray(obs[14], dtype=np.float32)

    if has_images:
        img = np.asarray(obs[n_obs - 1])
        if img.ndim >= 2:
            obs[n_obs - 1] = np.ascontiguousarray(np.flip(img, axis=-1))

    for i in range(n_obs, len(obs)):
        obs[i] = _hflip_action(obs[i])

    return tuple(obs)


def _is_discrete_action(action) -> bool:
    """True when action is a scalar integer (DQN discrete index)."""
    arr = np.asarray(action)
    return arr.ndim == 0 and np.issubdtype(arr.dtype, np.integer)


def _hflip_discrete_action(action_idx):
    """Mirror the steering component inside a Yosh-style discrete action index.

    Index layout: steer_idx * (n_gas * n_brake) + gas_idx * n_brake + brake_idx.
    Mirroring steering: new_steer = n_steer - 1 - steer_idx (symmetric about 0).
    """
    from tmrl.custom.tm.utils.discrete_control import YOSH_N_BRAKE, YOSH_N_GAS

    n_steer = int(cfg.ALG_CONFIG.get("IQN_N_STEER_BINS", 13))
    idx = int(action_idx)
    gas_brake = YOSH_N_GAS * YOSH_N_BRAKE
    steer_idx = idx // gas_brake
    remainder = idx % gas_brake
    return np.int64((n_steer - 1 - steer_idx) * gas_brake + remainder)


def _hflip_action(action):
    """Negate the steering component (index 2) of an action array.

    Action order is [gas, brake, steer] (control_gamepad / send_control).
    Do NOT negate index 0 (gas) or index 1 (brake); only steer (index 2) flips.
    For discrete (DQN) actions, mirrors the steer index within the composite action.
    """
    if _is_discrete_action(action):
        return _hflip_discrete_action(action)
    action_arr = np.array(action, dtype=np.float32, copy=True)
    if len(action_arr) >= 3:
        action_arr[ACTION_STEER_INDEX] *= -1.0
    return action_arr


def fog_recency_resample(
    indices: tuple[int, ...] | list[int],
    buffer_len: int,
    decay_temperature: float = 3.0,
) -> tuple[int, ...]:
    """Forget-and-Grow (FoG) replay decay: resample *indices* with exponential recency bias.

    Each index *i* (representing its position in the circular buffer) is assigned a
    sampling weight  ``exp(decay_temperature * i / buffer_len)``.  More recent
    transitions (higher *i*) therefore receive exponentially higher probability,
    discouraging the agent from anchoring on the earliest, low-quality experiences
    ("primacy bias").

    Args:
        indices: Raw indices produced by the base ``sample_indices()`` call.
        buffer_len: Current number of valid items in the replay buffer.
        decay_temperature: Controls the steepness of the recency curve.
            Higher values forget old data more aggressively.  0 = uniform (no FoG).

    Returns:
        A tuple of resampled indices with the same length as *indices*.
    """
    if decay_temperature <= 0.0 or buffer_len <= 1 or len(indices) == 0:
        return tuple(indices)

    idx_arr = np.asarray(indices, dtype=np.int64)
    log_weights = decay_temperature * (idx_arr.astype(np.float64) / buffer_len)
    log_weights -= log_weights.max()
    weights = np.exp(log_weights)
    total = weights.sum()
    if total <= 0:
        return tuple(indices)
    probs = weights / total
    resampled = np.random.choice(idx_arr, size=len(idx_arr), replace=True, p=probs)
    return tuple(int(x) for x in resampled)


def _sample_with_hflip(memory, n_obs, has_curvature=False, has_images=False):
    """Sample from R2D2 memory with per-sequence horizontal flip augmentation.

    Flip probability is read from ALG config key ``HORIZONTAL_FLIP_P`` (default 0.5).
    The flip is applied uniformly to entire sequences so GRU state remains consistent.

    Interaction with PER_ALPHA (PLAYER_RUNS_PER_ALPHA): sampling uses the same
    sample_indices() as base R2D2 (reward-weighted episode choice when applicable).
    We only augment the content of the chosen transitions; we do not change which
    indices/episodes are selected. So when a low-reward episode (e.g. crash on a
    left turn) is drawn, flipping that sequence yields a mirrored "right turn"
    lesson with the same reward signal, doubling the learning from that episode.
    """
    indices = memory.sample_indices()

    if len(indices) == 0:
        n = len(memory)
        if n == 0:
            raise RuntimeError("Cannot sample from empty replay memory")
        batch_size = min(memory.batch_size, n)
        indices = tuple(random.sample(range(n), batch_size))

    seq_len = cfg.ALG_CONFIG.get("R2D2_SEQUENCE_LENGTH", 0)
    total = len(indices)
    if seq_len <= 0 or total % seq_len != 0:
        seq_len = total
    num_seq = total // seq_len

    flip_p = float(cfg.ALG_CONFIG.get("HORIZONTAL_FLIP_P", 0.5))
    pts = cfg.POINTS_NUMBER

    per_td = getattr(cfg, "PER_TD_ENABLED", False)
    is_weights = getattr(memory, "_last_per_is_weights", [])
    batch = []
    for seq_i in range(num_seq):
        do_flip = flip_p > 0 and random.random() < flip_p
        start = seq_i * seq_len
        for j in range(seq_len):
            prev_obs, new_act, rew, new_obs, terminated, truncated, info = memory[
                indices[start + j]
            ]
            if do_flip:
                prev_obs = _hflip_tqcgrab_obs(prev_obs, n_obs, pts, has_curvature, has_images)
                new_obs = _hflip_tqcgrab_obs(new_obs, n_obs, pts, has_curvature, has_images)
                new_act = _hflip_action(new_act)
            if per_td and isinstance(info, dict):
                info = {**info, "batch_indices": indices[start + j]}
                pos = start + j
                if len(is_weights) == len(indices):
                    info["is_weight"] = np.float32(is_weights[pos])
            batch.append((prev_obs, new_act, rew, new_obs, terminated, truncated, info))

    return memory.collate(batch, memory.device)


# SUPPORTED CUSTOM MEMORIES ================================================================


class GenericTorchMemory(TorchMemory):
    def __init__(
        self,
        memory_size=1e6,
        batch_size=1,
        dataset_path="",
        nb_steps=1,
        sample_preprocessor: Callable[..., Any] | None = None,
        crc_debug=False,
        device="cpu",
    ):
        super().__init__(
            memory_size=memory_size,
            batch_size=batch_size,
            dataset_path=dataset_path,
            nb_steps=nb_steps,
            sample_preprocessor=sample_preprocessor,
            crc_debug=crc_debug,
            device=device,
        )
        self._valid_indices: list[int] = []
        if len(self.data) > 0 and len(self.data[GenericBufferSlot.ACTIONS]) > 1:
            n = len(self.data[GenericBufferSlot.ACTIONS]) - 1
            self._valid_indices = [i for i in range(n) if not self.data[GenericBufferSlot.DONE][i]]

    def append_buffer(self, buffer):
        slots = GenericBufferSlot
        actions_list = [b[0] for b in buffer.memory]
        obs_list = [b[1] for b in buffer.memory]
        rewards_list = [b[2] for b in buffer.memory]
        terminated_list = [b[3] for b in buffer.memory]
        truncated_list = [b[4] for b in buffer.memory]
        info_list = [b[5] for b in buffer.memory]
        done_list = [b[3] or b[4] for b in buffer.memory]

        if len(self.data) > 0:
            self.data[slots.ACTIONS] += actions_list
            self.data[slots.OBS] += obs_list
            self.data[slots.REWARDS] += rewards_list
            self.data[slots.TERMINATED] += terminated_list
            self.data[slots.TRUNCATED] += truncated_list
            self.data[slots.INFO] += info_list
            self.data[slots.DONE] += done_list
        else:
            self.data.append(actions_list)
            self.data.append(obs_list)
            self.data.append(rewards_list)
            self.data.append(terminated_list)
            self.data.append(truncated_list)
            self.data.append(info_list)
            self.data.append(done_list)

        count = len(self.data[slots.ACTIONS]) - 1
        to_trim = int(max(0, count - self.memory_size))
        if to_trim > 0:
            for slot in GenericBufferSlot:
                self.data[slot] = self.data[slot][to_trim:]

        count = len(self.data[slots.ACTIONS]) - 1
        self._valid_indices = [i for i in range(count) if not self.data[slots.DONE][i]]

    def __len__(self):
        return len(self._valid_indices)

    def get_transition(self, item):
        slots = GenericBufferSlot
        real_item = self._valid_indices[item]
        idx_last = real_item
        idx_now = real_item + 1

        last_obs = self.data[slots.OBS][idx_last]
        new_act = self.data[slots.ACTIONS][idx_now]
        rew = self.data[slots.REWARDS][idx_now]
        new_obs = self.data[slots.OBS][idx_now]
        terminated = self.data[slots.TERMINATED][idx_now]
        truncated = self.data[slots.TRUNCATED][idx_now]
        info = self.data[slots.INFO][idx_now]

        return last_obs, new_act, rew, new_obs, terminated, truncated, info


class MemoryTM(TorchMemory):
    def __init__(
        self,
        memory_size=None,
        batch_size=None,
        dataset_path="",
        imgs_obs=4,
        act_buf_len=1,
        nb_steps=1,
        sample_preprocessor: Callable[..., Any] | None = None,
        crc_debug=False,
        device="cpu",
    ):
        self.imgs_obs = imgs_obs
        self.act_buf_len = act_buf_len
        self.min_samples = max(self.imgs_obs, self.act_buf_len)
        self.start_imgs_offset = max(0, self.min_samples - self.imgs_obs)
        self.start_acts_offset = max(0, self.min_samples - self.act_buf_len)
        super().__init__(
            memory_size=memory_size,
            batch_size=batch_size,
            dataset_path=dataset_path,
            nb_steps=nb_steps,
            sample_preprocessor=sample_preprocessor,
            crc_debug=crc_debug,
            device=device,
        )
        """
        Arguments:

        memory_size: Maximum size of the memory buffer.
        batch_size: Size of batches used during training.
        dataset_path: Path to the dataset.
        imgs_obs: Number of observed images.
        act_buf_len: Length of the action buffer.
        nb_steps: Number of steps.
        sample_preprocessor: A callable function for sample preprocessing.
        crc_debug: Flag indicating whether to debug CRC (Cyclic Redundancy Check).
        device: Device where the memory is stored (e.g., "cpu" or "cuda").
        """

    def append_buffer(self, buffer):
        """
        Intended to be implemented in subclasses, not in MemoryTM itself.
        Raises NotImplementedError; subclasses must implement based on their requirements.
        """
        raise NotImplementedError

    def __len__(self):
        """
        Length of the memory buffer (number of samples).
        Returns 0 if no data; else length of data minus min_samples minus 1.
        """
        if len(self.data) == 0:
            return 0
        res = len(self.data[0]) - self.min_samples - 1
        if res < 0:
            return 0
        else:
            return res

    def get_transition(self, item):
        """
        Similar to append_buffer; to be implemented in subclasses, not in MemoryTM itself.
        Raises NotImplementedError; subclasses must implement based on their requirements.
        """
        raise NotImplementedError


class MemoryTMLidar(MemoryTM):
    def get_transition(self, item):
        """
        CAUTION: item is the first index of the 4 images in the OLD observation's image history.
        CAUTION: in the buffer, a sample is (act, obs(act)) and NOT (obs, act(obs))
            i.e. the observation is what step returned after being fed act (and preprocessed);
            in RTRL, act is appended to obs.
        So we load 5 images from here...
        Don't forget the info dict for CRC debugging
        """
        slot = MemoryTMLidarSlot
        if self.data[slot.EOES][item + self.min_samples - 1]:
            if item == 0:  # if first item of the buffer
                item += 1
            elif item == self.__len__() - 1:  # if last item of the buffer
                item -= 1
            elif random.random() < EOE_AVOID_MIDDLE_PROBABILITY:
                item += 1
            else:
                item -= 1

        idx_last = item + self.min_samples - 1
        idx_now = item + self.min_samples

        acts = self.load_acts(item)
        last_act_buf = acts[:-1]
        new_act_buf = acts[1:]

        imgs = self.load_imgs(item)
        imgs_last_obs = imgs[:-1]
        imgs_new_obs = imgs[1:]

        last_eoes = self.data[slot.EOES][idx_now - self.min_samples : idx_now]
        last_eoe_idx = last_true_in_list(last_eoes)

        assert last_eoe_idx is None or last_eoes[last_eoe_idx], f"last_eoe_idx:{last_eoe_idx}"

        if last_eoe_idx is not None:
            replace_hist_before_eoe(
                hist=new_act_buf, eoe_idx_in_hist=last_eoe_idx - self.start_acts_offset - 1
            )
            replace_hist_before_eoe(
                hist=last_act_buf, eoe_idx_in_hist=last_eoe_idx - self.start_acts_offset
            )
            replace_hist_before_eoe(
                hist=imgs_new_obs, eoe_idx_in_hist=last_eoe_idx - self.start_imgs_offset - 1
            )
            replace_hist_before_eoe(
                hist=imgs_last_obs, eoe_idx_in_hist=last_eoe_idx - self.start_imgs_offset
            )

        imgs_new_obs = np.ndarray.flatten(imgs_new_obs)
        imgs_last_obs = np.ndarray.flatten(imgs_last_obs)

        last_obs = (self.data[slot.SPEEDS][idx_last], imgs_last_obs, *last_act_buf)
        new_act = self.data[slot.ACTIONS][idx_now]
        rew = np.float32(self.data[slot.REWARDS][idx_now])
        new_obs = (self.data[slot.SPEEDS][idx_now], imgs_new_obs, *new_act_buf)
        terminated = self.data[slot.TERMINATED][idx_now]
        truncated = self.data[slot.TRUNCATED][idx_now]
        info = self.data[slot.INFOS][idx_now]
        return last_obs, new_act, rew, new_obs, terminated, truncated, info

    def load_imgs(self, item):
        slot = MemoryTMLidarSlot
        res = self.data[slot.LIDAR][
            (item + self.start_imgs_offset) : (item + self.start_imgs_offset + self.imgs_obs + 1)
        ]
        return np.stack(res)

    def load_acts(self, item):
        slot = MemoryTMLidarSlot
        res = self.data[slot.ACTIONS][
            (item + self.start_acts_offset) : (item + self.start_acts_offset + self.act_buf_len + 1)
        ]
        return res

    def append_buffer(self, buffer):
        """
        buffer is a list of samples (act, obs, rew, terminated, truncated, info)
        don't forget to keep the info dictionary in the sample for CRC debugging
        """
        slot = MemoryTMLidarSlot
        first_data_idx = self.data[slot.INDEX][-1] + 1 if self.__len__() > 0 else 0

        indices_list = [first_data_idx + i for i, _ in enumerate(buffer.memory)]
        actions_list = [b[0] for b in buffer.memory]
        speeds_list = [b[1][0] for b in buffer.memory]
        lidar_list = [b[1][1] for b in buffer.memory]
        eoes_list = [b[3] or b[4] for b in buffer.memory]
        rewards_list = [b[2] for b in buffer.memory]
        infos_list = [b[5] for b in buffer.memory]
        terminated_list = [b[3] for b in buffer.memory]
        truncated_list = [b[4] for b in buffer.memory]

        if self.__len__() > 0:
            self.data[slot.INDEX] += indices_list
            self.data[slot.ACTIONS] += actions_list
            self.data[slot.SPEEDS] += speeds_list
            self.data[slot.LIDAR] += lidar_list
            self.data[slot.EOES] += eoes_list
            self.data[slot.REWARDS] += rewards_list
            self.data[slot.INFOS] += infos_list
            self.data[slot.TERMINATED] += terminated_list
            self.data[slot.TRUNCATED] += truncated_list
        else:
            self.data.append(indices_list)
            self.data.append(actions_list)
            self.data.append(speeds_list)
            self.data.append(lidar_list)
            self.data.append(eoes_list)
            self.data.append(rewards_list)
            self.data.append(infos_list)
            self.data.append(terminated_list)
            self.data.append(truncated_list)

        to_trim = self.__len__() - self.memory_size
        if to_trim > 0:
            for slot_enum in MemoryTMLidarSlot:
                self.data[slot_enum] = self.data[slot_enum][to_trim:]

        return self


class MemoryTMLidarProgress(MemoryTM):
    def get_transition(self, item):
        """
        CAUTION: item is the first index of the 4 images in the OLD observation's image history.
        CAUTION: in the buffer, a sample is (act, obs(act)) and NOT (obs, act(obs))
            i.e. the observation is what step returned after being fed act (and preprocessed);
            in RTRL, act is appended to obs.
        So we load 5 images from here...
        Don't forget the info dict for CRC debugging
        """
        slot = MemoryTMLidarProgressSlot
        if self.data[slot.EOES][item + self.min_samples - 1]:
            if item == 0:
                item += 1
            elif item == self.__len__() - 1:
                item -= 1
            elif random.random() < EOE_AVOID_MIDDLE_PROBABILITY:
                item += 1
            else:
                item -= 1

        idx_last = item + self.min_samples - 1
        idx_now = item + self.min_samples

        acts = self.load_acts(item)
        last_act_buf = acts[:-1]
        new_act_buf = acts[1:]

        imgs = self.load_imgs(item)
        imgs_last_obs = imgs[:-1]
        imgs_new_obs = imgs[1:]

        last_eoes = self.data[slot.EOES][idx_now - self.min_samples : idx_now]
        last_eoe_idx = last_true_in_list(last_eoes)

        assert last_eoe_idx is None or last_eoes[last_eoe_idx], f"last_eoe_idx:{last_eoe_idx}"

        if last_eoe_idx is not None:
            replace_hist_before_eoe(
                hist=new_act_buf, eoe_idx_in_hist=last_eoe_idx - self.start_acts_offset - 1
            )
            replace_hist_before_eoe(
                hist=last_act_buf, eoe_idx_in_hist=last_eoe_idx - self.start_acts_offset
            )
            replace_hist_before_eoe(
                hist=imgs_new_obs, eoe_idx_in_hist=last_eoe_idx - self.start_imgs_offset - 1
            )
            replace_hist_before_eoe(
                hist=imgs_last_obs, eoe_idx_in_hist=last_eoe_idx - self.start_imgs_offset
            )

        imgs_new_obs = np.ndarray.flatten(imgs_new_obs)
        imgs_last_obs = np.ndarray.flatten(imgs_last_obs)

        last_obs = (
            self.data[slot.SPEEDS][idx_last],
            self.data[slot.PROGRESS][idx_last],
            imgs_last_obs,
            *last_act_buf,
        )
        new_act = self.data[slot.ACTIONS][idx_now]
        rew = np.float32(self.data[slot.REWARDS][idx_now])
        new_obs = (
            self.data[slot.SPEEDS][idx_now],
            self.data[slot.PROGRESS][idx_now],
            imgs_new_obs,
            *new_act_buf,
        )
        terminated = self.data[slot.TERMINATED][idx_now]
        truncated = self.data[slot.TRUNCATED][idx_now]
        info = self.data[slot.INFOS][idx_now]
        return last_obs, new_act, rew, new_obs, terminated, truncated, info

    def load_imgs(self, item):
        slot = MemoryTMLidarProgressSlot
        res = self.data[slot.LIDAR][
            (item + self.start_imgs_offset) : (item + self.start_imgs_offset + self.imgs_obs + 1)
        ]
        return np.stack(res)

    def load_acts(self, item):
        slot = MemoryTMLidarProgressSlot
        res = self.data[slot.ACTIONS][
            (item + self.start_acts_offset) : (item + self.start_acts_offset + self.act_buf_len + 1)
        ]
        return res

    def append_buffer(self, buffer):
        """
        buffer is a list of samples (act, obs, rew, truncated, terminated, info)
        don't forget to keep the info dictionary in the sample for CRC debugging
        """
        slot = MemoryTMLidarProgressSlot
        first_data_idx = self.data[slot.INDEX][-1] + 1 if self.__len__() > 0 else 0

        indices_list = [first_data_idx + i for i, _ in enumerate(buffer.memory)]
        actions_list = [b[0] for b in buffer.memory]
        speeds_list = [b[1][0] for b in buffer.memory]
        lidar_list = [b[1][2] for b in buffer.memory]
        eoes_list = [b[3] or b[4] for b in buffer.memory]
        rewards_list = [b[2] for b in buffer.memory]
        infos_list = [b[5] for b in buffer.memory]
        progress_list = [b[1][1] for b in buffer.memory]
        terminated_list = [b[3] for b in buffer.memory]
        truncated_list = [b[4] for b in buffer.memory]

        slot_data_lists = (
            indices_list,
            actions_list,
            speeds_list,
            lidar_list,
            eoes_list,
            rewards_list,
            infos_list,
            progress_list,
            terminated_list,
            truncated_list,
        )
        if self.__len__() > 0:
            for slot_enum, slot_data in zip(MemoryTMLidarProgressSlot, slot_data_lists):
                self.data[slot_enum] += slot_data
        else:
            self.data.extend(slot_data_lists)

        to_trim = self.__len__() - self.memory_size
        if to_trim > 0:
            for slot_enum in MemoryTMLidarProgressSlot:
                self.data[slot_enum] = self.data[slot_enum][to_trim:]

        return self


class MemoryTMLidarProgressImages(MemoryTM):
    """Replay memory for (speed, progress, lidar, images). One transition = two steps.
    When imgs_obs > 1 we store only the newest frame per step; history in get_transition.
    """

    def _load_img_hist(self, end_idx):
        """Load image history (imgs_obs, ...) for step at end_idx, padding at start if needed."""
        slot = MemoryTMLidarProgressImagesSlot
        start = max(0, end_idx - self.imgs_obs + 1)
        imgs = list(self.data[slot.IMAGES][start : end_idx + 1])
        if len(imgs) < self.imgs_obs and imgs:
            first = np.array(imgs[0], copy=True)
            while len(imgs) < self.imgs_obs:
                imgs.insert(0, first)
        return np.array(imgs, dtype=np.float32)

    def get_transition(self, item):
        slot = MemoryTMLidarProgressImagesSlot
        if self.data[slot.EOES][item + 1]:
            if item == 0:
                item += 1
            elif item == self.__len__() - 1:
                item -= 1
            elif random.random() < EOE_AVOID_MIDDLE_PROBABILITY:
                item += 1
            else:
                item -= 1

        idx_last = item
        idx_now = item + 1

        last_imgs = self._load_img_hist(idx_last)
        new_imgs = self._load_img_hist(idx_now)

        last_obs = (
            self.data[slot.SPEEDS][idx_last],
            self.data[slot.PROGRESS][idx_last],
            self.data[slot.LIDAR][idx_last],
            last_imgs,
        )
        new_act = self.data[slot.ACTIONS][idx_now]
        rew = np.float32(self.data[slot.REWARDS][idx_now])
        new_obs = (
            self.data[slot.SPEEDS][idx_now],
            self.data[slot.PROGRESS][idx_now],
            self.data[slot.LIDAR][idx_now],
            new_imgs,
        )
        terminated = self.data[slot.TERMINATED][idx_now]
        truncated = self.data[slot.TRUNCATED][idx_now]
        info = self.data[slot.INFOS][idx_now]
        return last_obs, new_act, rew, new_obs, terminated, truncated, info

    def append_buffer(self, buffer):
        slot = MemoryTMLidarProgressImagesSlot
        first_data_idx = self.data[slot.INDEX][-1] + 1 if self.__len__() > 0 else 0

        # Store single image per step when obs has a stack (saves memory for IMG_HIST_LEN > 1)
        def _single_frame(imgs):
            return imgs[-1] if getattr(imgs, "ndim", 0) >= 3 and imgs.shape[0] > 1 else imgs

        slot_data_lists = [
            [first_data_idx + i for i, _ in enumerate(buffer.memory)],
            [b[0] for b in buffer.memory],
            [b[1][0] for b in buffer.memory],
            [b[1][1] for b in buffer.memory],
            [b[1][2] for b in buffer.memory],
            [_single_frame(b[1][3]) for b in buffer.memory],
            [b[3] or b[4] for b in buffer.memory],
            [b[2] for b in buffer.memory],
            [b[5] for b in buffer.memory],
            [b[3] for b in buffer.memory],
            [b[4] for b in buffer.memory],
        ]

        if self.__len__() > 0:
            for slot_enum, slot_data in zip(MemoryTMLidarProgressImagesSlot, slot_data_lists):
                self.data[slot_enum] += slot_data
        else:
            self.data = slot_data_lists

        to_trim = len(self.data[slot.INDEX]) - self.memory_size
        if to_trim > 0:
            for slot_enum in MemoryTMLidarProgressImagesSlot:
                self.data[slot_enum] = self.data[slot_enum][to_trim:]
        return self


class MemoryTMFull(MemoryTM):
    def __init__(
        self,
        memory_size=None,
        batch_size=None,
        dataset_path="",
        imgs_obs=4,
        act_buf_len=1,
        nb_steps=1,
        sample_preprocessor: Callable[..., Any] | None = None,
        crc_debug=False,
        device="cpu",
    ):
        super().__init__(
            memory_size=memory_size,
            batch_size=batch_size,
            dataset_path=dataset_path,
            imgs_obs=imgs_obs,
            act_buf_len=act_buf_len,
            nb_steps=nb_steps,
            sample_preprocessor=sample_preprocessor,
            crc_debug=crc_debug,
            device=device,
        )
        n_slots = len(MemoryTMFullSlot)
        if len(self.data) == 0:
            self.data = [deque(maxlen=self.memory_size) for _ in range(n_slots)]
        else:
            self.data = [deque(slot_deque, maxlen=self.memory_size) for slot_deque in self.data]

    def get_transition(self, item):
        """
        CAUTION: item is the first index of the 4 images in the OLD observation's image history.
        CAUTION: in the buffer, a sample is (act, obs(act)) and NOT (obs, act(obs))
            i.e. the observation is what step returned after being fed act (and preprocessed);
            in RTRL, act is appended to obs.
        So we load 5 images from here...
        Don't forget the info dict for CRC debugging
        """
        slot = MemoryTMFullSlot
        if self.data[slot.EOES][item + self.min_samples - 1]:
            if item == 0:
                item += 1
            elif item == self.__len__() - 1:
                item -= 1
            elif random.random() < EOE_AVOID_MIDDLE_PROBABILITY:
                item += 1
            else:
                item -= 1

        idx_last = item + self.min_samples - 1
        idx_now = item + self.min_samples

        acts = self.load_acts(item)
        last_act_buf = acts[:-1]
        new_act_buf = acts[1:]

        imgs = self.load_imgs(item)
        imgs_last_obs = imgs[:-1]
        imgs_new_obs = imgs[1:]

        last_eoes = self.data[slot.EOES][idx_now - self.min_samples : idx_now]
        last_eoe_idx = last_true_in_list(last_eoes)

        assert last_eoe_idx is None or last_eoes[last_eoe_idx], f"last_eoe_idx:{last_eoe_idx}"

        if last_eoe_idx is not None:
            replace_hist_before_eoe(
                hist=new_act_buf, eoe_idx_in_hist=last_eoe_idx - self.start_acts_offset - 1
            )
            replace_hist_before_eoe(
                hist=last_act_buf, eoe_idx_in_hist=last_eoe_idx - self.start_acts_offset
            )
            replace_hist_before_eoe(
                hist=imgs_new_obs, eoe_idx_in_hist=last_eoe_idx - self.start_imgs_offset - 1
            )
            replace_hist_before_eoe(
                hist=imgs_last_obs, eoe_idx_in_hist=last_eoe_idx - self.start_imgs_offset
            )

        last_obs = (
            self.data[slot.SPEEDS][idx_last],
            self.data[slot.GEARS][idx_last],
            self.data[slot.RPMS][idx_last],
            imgs_last_obs,
            *last_act_buf,
        )
        new_act = self.data[slot.ACTIONS][idx_now]
        rew = np.float32(self.data[slot.REWARDS][idx_now])
        new_obs = (
            self.data[slot.SPEEDS][idx_now],
            self.data[slot.GEARS][idx_now],
            self.data[slot.RPMS][idx_now],
            imgs_new_obs,
            *new_act_buf,
        )
        terminated = self.data[slot.TERMINATED][idx_now]
        truncated = self.data[slot.TRUNCATED][idx_now]
        info = self.data[slot.INFOS][idx_now]
        return last_obs, new_act, rew, new_obs, terminated, truncated, info

    def load_imgs(self, item):
        slot = MemoryTMFullSlot
        res = self.data[slot.IMAGES][
            (item + self.start_imgs_offset) : (item + self.start_imgs_offset + self.imgs_obs + 1)
        ]
        return np.stack(res).astype(np.float32) / 256.0

    def load_acts(self, item):
        slot = MemoryTMFullSlot
        res = self.data[slot.ACTIONS][
            (item + self.start_acts_offset) : (item + self.start_acts_offset + self.act_buf_len + 1)
        ]
        return res

    def append_buffer(self, buffer):
        """
        buffer is a list of samples ( act, obs, rew, terminated, truncated, info)
        don't forget to keep the info dictionary in the sample for CRC debugging
        When imgs_obs > 1 we store only the newest frame per step to avoid duplicate
        image storage; load_imgs() reconstructs (imgs_obs+1) frames from consecutive indices.
        """
        slot = MemoryTMFullSlot
        first_data_idx = self.data[slot.INDEX][-1] + 1 if self.__len__() > 0 else 0

        # Store single image per step when obs has a stack (saves memory for IMG_HIST_LEN > 1)
        def _single_frame(imgs):
            return imgs[-1] if getattr(imgs, "ndim", 0) == 3 else imgs

        slot_data_lists = [
            [first_data_idx + i for i, _ in enumerate(buffer.memory)],
            [b[0] for b in buffer.memory],
            [b[1][0] for b in buffer.memory],
            [_single_frame(b[1][3]) for b in buffer.memory],
            [b[3] or b[4] for b in buffer.memory],
            [b[2] for b in buffer.memory],
            [b[5] for b in buffer.memory],
            [b[1][1] for b in buffer.memory],
            [b[1][2] for b in buffer.memory],
            [b[3] for b in buffer.memory],
            [b[4] for b in buffer.memory],
        ]

        # Deques handle maxlen automatically, no slicing needed
        for slot_enum, slot_data in zip(MemoryTMFullSlot, slot_data_lists):
            self.data[slot_enum].extend(slot_data)

        return self


# ============= custom mobilenet memory ==============


class MemoryTMBest(MemoryTM):
    def __init__(
        self,
        memory_size=None,
        batch_size=None,
        dataset_path="",
        imgs_obs=4,
        act_buf_len=1,
        nb_steps=1,
        sample_preprocessor: Callable[..., Any] | None = None,
        crc_debug=False,
        device="cpu",
    ):
        super().__init__(
            memory_size=memory_size,
            batch_size=batch_size,
            dataset_path=dataset_path,
            imgs_obs=imgs_obs,
            act_buf_len=act_buf_len,
            nb_steps=nb_steps,
            sample_preprocessor=sample_preprocessor,
            crc_debug=crc_debug,
            device=device,
        )
        n_slots = len(MemoryTMBestSlot)
        if len(self.data) == 0:
            self.data = [deque(maxlen=self.memory_size) for _ in range(n_slots)]
        else:
            self.data = [deque(slot_deque, maxlen=self.memory_size) for slot_deque in self.data]

    def get_transition(self, item):
        """
        CAUTION: item is the first index of the 4 images in the OLD observation's image history.
        CAUTION: in the buffer, a sample is (act, obs(act)) and NOT (obs, act(obs))
            i.e. the observation is what step returned after being fed act (and preprocessed);
            in RTRL, act is appended to obs.
        So we load 5 images from here...
        Don't forget the info dict for CRC debugging
        """
        idx_last = item + self.min_samples - 1
        idx_now = item + self.min_samples

        acts = self.load_acts(item)
        last_act_buf = acts[:-1]
        new_act_buf = acts[1:]

        imgs = self.load_imgs(item)
        imgs_last_obs = imgs[:-1]
        imgs_new_obs = imgs[1:]

        slot = MemoryTMBestSlot
        last_eoes = self.data[slot.EOES][idx_now - self.min_samples : idx_now]
        last_eoe_idx = last_true_in_list(last_eoes)

        assert last_eoe_idx is None or last_eoes[last_eoe_idx], f"last_eoe_idx:{last_eoe_idx}"

        if last_eoe_idx is not None:
            replace_hist_before_eoe(
                hist=new_act_buf, eoe_idx_in_hist=last_eoe_idx - self.start_acts_offset - 1
            )
            replace_hist_before_eoe(
                hist=last_act_buf, eoe_idx_in_hist=last_eoe_idx - self.start_acts_offset
            )
            replace_hist_before_eoe(
                hist=imgs_new_obs, eoe_idx_in_hist=last_eoe_idx - self.start_imgs_offset - 1
            )
            replace_hist_before_eoe(
                hist=imgs_last_obs, eoe_idx_in_hist=last_eoe_idx - self.start_imgs_offset
            )

        last_obs = (
            self.data[slot.POSITIONS][idx_last],
            self.data[slot.SPEEDS][idx_last],
            self.data[slot.ACCELERATION][idx_last],
            self.data[slot.JERK][idx_last],
            self.data[slot.RACE_PROGRESS][idx_last],
            self.data[slot.INPUT_STEER][idx_last],
            self.data[slot.INPUT_GAS_PEDAL][idx_last],
            self.data[slot.INPUT_BRAKE][idx_last],
            self.data[slot.GEAR][idx_last],
            self.data[slot.RPM][idx_last],
            self.data[slot.AIM_YAW][idx_last],
            self.data[slot.AIM_PITCH][idx_last],
            self.data[slot.SURFACE_ID][idx_last],
            self.data[slot.STEER_ANGLE][idx_last],
            self.data[slot.WHEEL_ROT][idx_last],
            self.data[slot.WHEEL_ROT_SPEED][idx_last],
            self.data[slot.DAMPER_LEN][idx_last],
            self.data[slot.SLIP_COEF][idx_last],
            self.data[slot.REACTOR_GROUND_MODE][idx_last],
            self.data[slot.GROUND_CONTACT][idx_last],
            self.data[slot.REACTOR_AIR_CONTROL][idx_last],
            self.data[slot.GROUND_DIST][idx_last],
            self.data[slot.CRASHED][idx_last],
            self.data[slot.FAILURE_COUNTER][idx_last],
            self.data[slot.IMGS][idx_last],
            *last_act_buf,
        )
        new_act = self.data[slot.ACTIONS][idx_now]
        rew = np.float32(self.data[slot.REWARDS][idx_now])
        new_obs = (
            self.data[slot.POSITIONS][idx_now],
            self.data[slot.SPEEDS][idx_now],
            self.data[slot.ACCELERATION][idx_now],
            self.data[slot.JERK][idx_now],
            self.data[slot.RACE_PROGRESS][idx_now],
            self.data[slot.INPUT_STEER][idx_now],
            self.data[slot.INPUT_GAS_PEDAL][idx_now],
            self.data[slot.INPUT_BRAKE][idx_now],
            self.data[slot.GEAR][idx_now],
            self.data[slot.RPM][idx_now],
            self.data[slot.AIM_YAW][idx_now],
            self.data[slot.AIM_PITCH][idx_now],
            self.data[slot.SURFACE_ID][idx_now],
            self.data[slot.STEER_ANGLE][idx_now],
            self.data[slot.WHEEL_ROT][idx_now],
            self.data[slot.WHEEL_ROT_SPEED][idx_now],
            self.data[slot.DAMPER_LEN][idx_now],
            self.data[slot.SLIP_COEF][idx_now],
            self.data[slot.REACTOR_GROUND_MODE][idx_now],
            self.data[slot.GROUND_CONTACT][idx_now],
            self.data[slot.REACTOR_AIR_CONTROL][idx_now],
            self.data[slot.GROUND_DIST][idx_now],
            self.data[slot.CRASHED][idx_now],
            self.data[slot.FAILURE_COUNTER][idx_now],
            self.data[slot.IMGS][idx_now],
            *new_act_buf,
        )
        terminated = self.data[slot.TERMINATED][idx_now]
        truncated = self.data[slot.TRUNCATED][idx_now]
        info = self.data[slot.INFOS][idx_now]
        return last_obs, new_act, rew, new_obs, terminated, truncated, info

    def load_imgs(self, item):
        slot = MemoryTMBestSlot
        res = self.data[slot.IMGS][
            (item + self.start_imgs_offset) : (item + self.start_imgs_offset + self.imgs_obs + 1)
        ]
        return np.stack(res).astype(np.float32) / 256.0

    def load_acts(self, item):
        slot = MemoryTMBestSlot
        res = self.data[slot.ACTIONS][
            (item + self.start_acts_offset) : (item + self.start_acts_offset + self.act_buf_len + 1)
        ]
        return res

    def append_buffer(self, buffer):
        """
        buffer is a list of samples ( act, obs, rew, terminated, truncated, info)
        don't forget to keep the info dictionary in the sample for CRC debugging
        """
        slot = MemoryTMBestSlot
        first_data_idx = self.data[slot.INDEX][-1] + 1 if self.__len__() > 0 else 0

        reactor_air_control_list = [b[1][22].tolist() for b in buffer.memory]
        slot_data_lists = [
            [first_data_idx + i for i, _ in enumerate(buffer.memory)],
            [b[0] for b in buffer.memory],
            [np.array([b[1][0]]) for b in buffer.memory],
            [np.array([b[1][1]]) for b in buffer.memory],
            [np.array([b[1][2]]) for b in buffer.memory],
            [np.array([b[1][3]]) for b in buffer.memory],
            [np.array([b[1][4]]) for b in buffer.memory],
            [np.array([b[1][5]]) for b in buffer.memory],
            [np.array([b[1][6]]) for b in buffer.memory],
            [np.array([b[1][7]]) for b in buffer.memory],
            [np.array([b[1][8]]) for b in buffer.memory],
            [np.array([b[1][9]]) for b in buffer.memory],
            [np.array([b[1][10]]) for b in buffer.memory],
            [np.array([b[1][11]]) for b in buffer.memory],
            [np.array([b[1][12][0]]) for b in buffer.memory],
            [np.array([b[1][13][0]]) for b in buffer.memory],
            [np.array([b[1][14][0]]) for b in buffer.memory],
            [np.array([b[1][15][0]]) for b in buffer.memory],
            [np.array(b[1][16]) for b in buffer.memory],
            [np.array(b[1][17]) for b in buffer.memory],
            [np.array([b[1][18]]) for b in buffer.memory],
            [np.array([b[1][19]]) for b in buffer.memory],
            [np.array(b[1][20]) for b in buffer.memory],
            [np.array([b[1][21]]) for b in buffer.memory],
            [np.array([el]) for el in reactor_air_control_list],
            [np.array([b[1][23]]) for b in buffer.memory],
            [b[1][24] for b in buffer.memory],
            [b[3] or b[4] for b in buffer.memory],
            [b[2] for b in buffer.memory],
            [b[5] for b in buffer.memory],
            [b[3] for b in buffer.memory],
            [b[4] for b in buffer.memory],
        ]

        # Deques handle maxlen automatically, no slicing needed
        for slot_enum, slot_data in zip(MemoryTMBestSlot, slot_data_lists):
            self.data[slot_enum].extend(slot_data)

        return self


# ============================= R2D2 MEMORY ===============================


class MemoryR2D2(R2D2Memory):
    def __init__(
        self,
        memory_size=None,
        batch_size=None,
        dataset_path="",
        imgs_obs=4,
        act_buf_len=1,
        nb_steps=2,
        sample_preprocessor: Callable[..., Any] | None = None,
        crc_debug=False,
        device="cpu",
    ):
        self.imgs_obs = imgs_obs
        self.act_buf_len = act_buf_len
        self.min_samples = max(self.imgs_obs, self.act_buf_len)
        self.start_imgs_offset = max(0, self.min_samples - self.imgs_obs)
        self.start_acts_offset = max(0, self.min_samples - self.act_buf_len)

        super().__init__(
            memory_size=memory_size,
            batch_size=batch_size,
            dataset_path=dataset_path,
            nb_steps=nb_steps,
            sample_preprocessor=sample_preprocessor,
            crc_debug=crc_debug,
            device=device,
        )
        n_slots = len(MemoryR2D2Slot)
        if len(self.data) == 0:
            self.data = [deque(maxlen=self.memory_size) for _ in range(n_slots)]
        else:
            self.data = [deque(slot_deque, maxlen=self.memory_size) for slot_deque in self.data]

    def __len__(self):
        if len(self.data) == 0:
            return 0
        res = len(self.data[0]) - self.min_samples - 1
        if res < 0:
            return 0
        else:
            return res

    def get_transition(self, item):
        """
        CAUTION: item is the first index of the 4 images in the OLD observation's image history.
        CAUTION: in the buffer, a sample is (act, obs(act)) and NOT (obs, act(obs))
            i.e. the observation is what step returned after being fed act (and preprocessed);
            in RTRL, act is appended to obs.
        So we load 5 images from here...
        Don't forget the info dict for CRC debugging
        """
        idx_last = item + self.min_samples - 1
        idx_now = item + self.min_samples

        acts = self.load_acts(item)
        last_act_buf = acts[:-1]
        new_act_buf = acts[1:]

        imgs = self.load_imgs(item)
        imgs_last_obs = imgs[:-1]
        imgs_new_obs = imgs[1:]

        slot = MemoryR2D2Slot
        last_eoes = self.data[slot.EOES][idx_now - self.min_samples : idx_now]
        last_eoe_idx = last_true_in_list(last_eoes)

        assert last_eoe_idx is None or last_eoes[last_eoe_idx], f"last_eoe_idx:{last_eoe_idx}"

        if last_eoe_idx is not None:
            replace_hist_before_eoe(
                hist=new_act_buf, eoe_idx_in_hist=last_eoe_idx - self.start_acts_offset - 1
            )
            replace_hist_before_eoe(
                hist=last_act_buf, eoe_idx_in_hist=last_eoe_idx - self.start_acts_offset
            )
            replace_hist_before_eoe(
                hist=imgs_new_obs, eoe_idx_in_hist=last_eoe_idx - self.start_imgs_offset - 1
            )
            replace_hist_before_eoe(
                hist=imgs_last_obs, eoe_idx_in_hist=last_eoe_idx - self.start_imgs_offset
            )

        last_obs = (
            self.data[slot.OBS_0][idx_last],
            self.data[slot.OBS_1][idx_last],
            self.data[slot.OBS_2][idx_last],
            self.data[slot.OBS_3][idx_last],
            self.data[slot.OBS_4][idx_last],
            self.data[slot.OBS_5][idx_last],
            self.data[slot.OBS_6][idx_last],
            self.data[slot.OBS_7][idx_last],
            self.data[slot.OBS_8][idx_last],
            self.data[slot.OBS_9][idx_last],
            self.data[slot.OBS_10][idx_last],
            self.data[slot.OBS_11][idx_last],
            self.data[slot.OBS_12][idx_last],
            self.data[slot.OBS_13][idx_last],
            self.data[slot.OBS_14][idx_last],
            *last_act_buf,
        )
        new_act = self.data[slot.ACTIONS][idx_now]
        rew = np.float32(self.data[slot.REWARDS][idx_now])
        new_obs = (
            self.data[slot.OBS_0][idx_now],
            self.data[slot.OBS_1][idx_now],
            self.data[slot.OBS_2][idx_now],
            self.data[slot.OBS_3][idx_now],
            self.data[slot.OBS_4][idx_now],
            self.data[slot.OBS_5][idx_now],
            self.data[slot.OBS_6][idx_now],
            self.data[slot.OBS_7][idx_now],
            self.data[slot.OBS_8][idx_now],
            self.data[slot.OBS_9][idx_now],
            self.data[slot.OBS_10][idx_now],
            self.data[slot.OBS_11][idx_now],
            self.data[slot.OBS_12][idx_now],
            self.data[slot.OBS_13][idx_now],
            self.data[slot.OBS_14][idx_now],
            *new_act_buf,
        )
        terminated = self.data[slot.TERMINATED][idx_now]
        truncated = self.data[slot.TRUNCATED][idx_now]
        info = self.data[slot.INFOS][idx_now]
        return last_obs, new_act, rew, new_obs, terminated, truncated, info

    def load_imgs(self, item):
        slot = MemoryR2D2Slot
        res = self.data[slot.OBS_14][
            (item + self.start_imgs_offset) : (item + self.start_imgs_offset + self.imgs_obs + 1)
        ]
        return np.stack(res).astype(np.float32) / 256.0

    def load_acts(self, item):
        slot = MemoryR2D2Slot
        res = self.data[slot.ACTIONS][
            (item + self.start_acts_offset) : (item + self.start_acts_offset + self.act_buf_len + 1)
        ]
        return res

    def append_buffer(self, buffer):
        """
        buffer is a list of samples ( act, obs, rew, terminated, truncated, info)
        don't forget to keep the info dictionary in the sample for CRC debugging
        When imgs_obs > 1 we store only the newest image frame in OBS_14 to save memory.
        """
        slot = MemoryR2D2Slot
        first_data_idx = self.data[slot.INDEX][-1] + 1 if self.__len__() > 0 else 0

        def _single_frame(imgs):
            return imgs[-1] if getattr(imgs, "ndim", 0) == 3 else np.array(imgs)

        slot_data_lists = [
            [first_data_idx + i for i, _ in enumerate(buffer.memory)],
            [b[0] for b in buffer.memory],
            [np.array(b[1][0]) for b in buffer.memory],
            [np.array(b[1][1]) for b in buffer.memory],
            [np.array(b[1][2]) for b in buffer.memory],
            [np.array(b[1][3]) for b in buffer.memory],
            [np.array(b[1][4]) for b in buffer.memory],
            [np.array(b[1][5]) for b in buffer.memory],
            [np.array(b[1][6]) for b in buffer.memory],
            [np.array(b[1][7]) for b in buffer.memory],
            [np.array(b[1][8]) for b in buffer.memory],
            [np.array(b[1][9]) for b in buffer.memory],
            [np.array(b[1][10]) for b in buffer.memory],
            [np.array(b[1][11]) for b in buffer.memory],
            [np.array(b[1][12]) for b in buffer.memory],
            [np.array(b[1][13]) for b in buffer.memory],
            [_single_frame(b[1][14]) for b in buffer.memory],
            [b[3] or b[4] for b in buffer.memory],
            [b[2] for b in buffer.memory],
            [b[5] for b in buffer.memory],
            [b[3] for b in buffer.memory],
            [b[4] for b in buffer.memory],
        ]

        # Deques handle maxlen automatically
        for slot_enum, slot_data in zip(MemoryR2D2Slot, slot_data_lists):
            self.data[slot_enum].extend(slot_data)
        n_new = len(slot_data_lists[0])
        self._extend_priorities(n_new)
        to_trim = len(self.priorities) - self.memory_size
        if to_trim > 0:
            self._trim_priorities(to_trim)

        return self


# ============================ Contiguous circular buffer (O(1) random access) ==================


class _CircularSlotView:
    """Read-only circular buffer slot view. O(1) __getitem__, __len__, __iter__.

    Stores a back-reference to the parent memory so that _ptr/_size are always
    read live. Using a direct reference (instead of lambdas) keeps pickle happy.
    """

    __slots__ = ("_arr", "_parent", "_max_cap")

    def __init__(self, arr, parent, max_cap):
        self._arr = arr
        self._parent = parent
        self._max_cap = max_cap

    def _storage_index(self, step_idx: int) -> int:
        ptr = self._parent._ptr
        size = self._parent._size
        if step_idx < 0 or step_idx >= size:
            raise IndexError(f"step_idx {step_idx} out of range [0, {size})")
        return int((ptr - size + step_idx) % self._max_cap)

    def __getitem__(self, step_idx: int):
        return self._arr[self._storage_index(step_idx)]

    def __len__(self) -> int:
        return int(self._parent._size)

    def __iter__(self):
        for i in range(len(self)):
            yield self[i]


# ============================ R2D2 MEMORY without Images ==================


class MemoryR2D2woImages(R2D2Memory):
    """R2D2 replay without images. Optional curvature as 15th obs (TRACK_CURVATURE_OBS)."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        memory_size=None,
        batch_size=None,
        dataset_path="",
        imgs_obs=4,
        act_buf_len=1,
        nb_steps=2,
        sample_preprocessor: Callable[..., Any] | None = None,
        crc_debug=False,
        device="cpu",
        use_curvature=False,
    ):
        self.imgs_obs = imgs_obs
        self.act_buf_len = act_buf_len
        self.use_curvature = bool(use_curvature)
        self.min_samples = max(self.imgs_obs, self.act_buf_len)
        self.start_imgs_offset = max(0, self.min_samples - self.imgs_obs)
        self.start_acts_offset = max(0, self.min_samples - self.act_buf_len)

        # Contiguous buffer attrs must exist before super().__init__ which calls len(self)
        self._use_contiguous = True
        self._ptr = 0
        self._size = 0
        self._max_cap = int(memory_size or 1000000)
        self._arrays = None

        super().__init__(
            memory_size=memory_size,
            batch_size=batch_size,
            dataset_path=dataset_path,
            nb_steps=nb_steps,
            sample_preprocessor=sample_preprocessor,
            crc_debug=crc_debug,
            device=device,
        )
        self._max_cap = int(self.memory_size)
        # 22-slot layout: info is at index 19 (18 in 21-slot layout)
        if self.use_curvature:
            self.rewards_index = 19
        # Loaded dataset must match curvature layout (22 slots when use_curvature, else 21)
        if self.use_curvature and len(self.data) > 0 and len(self.data) == 21:
            self.data = []
            import logging

            logging.getLogger("tmrl.custom.custom_memories").warning(
                "MemoryR2D2woImages: use_curvature=True but loaded dataset has 21 slots; cleared."
            )
        if len(self.data) == 0:
            self.data = []
        else:
            # Loaded from dataset: keep deques for compatibility
            self._use_contiguous = False
            self.data = [deque(slot_deque, maxlen=self.memory_size) for slot_deque in self.data]

    def __len__(self):
        if self._use_contiguous:
            if self._arrays is None or self._size == 0:
                return 0
            return max(0, self._size - self.min_samples - 1)
        if len(self.data) == 0:
            return 0
        return max(0, len(self.data[0]) - self.min_samples - 1)

    def _storage_index_wo(self, step_idx: int) -> int:
        """Map logical step index to circular buffer index (MemoryR2D2woImages)."""
        if step_idx < 0 or step_idx >= self._size:
            raise IndexError(f"step_idx {step_idx} out of range [0, {self._size})")
        return (self._ptr - self._size + step_idx) % self._max_cap

    def get_transition(self, item):
        """
        CAUTION: item is the first index of the 4 images in the OLD observation's image history.
        CAUTION: in the buffer, a sample is (act, obs(act)) and NOT (obs, act(obs))
            i.e. the observation is what step returned after being fed act (and preprocessed);
            in RTRL, act is appended to obs.
        So we load 5 images from here...
        Don't forget the info dict for CRC debugging
        """
        idx_last = item + self.min_samples - 1
        idx_now = item + self.min_samples

        acts = self.load_acts(item)
        last_act_buf = acts[:-1]
        new_act_buf = acts[1:]

        # use_curvature: False=21 slots (16=eoes,17=rew,...), True=22 (16=curv,17=eoes,18=rew,...)
        slot_rewards = 18 if self.use_curvature else 17
        slot_info = 19 if self.use_curvature else 18
        slot_terminated = 20 if self.use_curvature else 19
        slot_truncated = 21 if self.use_curvature else 20
        slot_obs_start = 2
        slot_obs_end = 16

        if self._use_contiguous and self._arrays is not None:
            s_last = self._storage_index_wo(idx_last)
            s_now = self._storage_index_wo(idx_now)
            arr = self._arrays
            base_last = (
                arr[slot_obs_start][s_last],
                arr[3][s_last],
                arr[4][s_last],
                arr[5][s_last],
                arr[6][s_last],
                arr[7][s_last],
                arr[8][s_last],
                arr[9][s_last],
                arr[10][s_last],
                arr[11][s_last],
                arr[12][s_last],
                arr[13][s_last],
                arr[14][s_last],
                arr[15][s_last],
            )
            if self.use_curvature:
                last_obs = (*base_last, arr[16][s_last], *last_act_buf)
                new_obs = (
                    arr[slot_obs_start][s_now],
                    arr[3][s_now],
                    arr[4][s_now],
                    arr[5][s_now],
                    arr[6][s_now],
                    arr[7][s_now],
                    arr[8][s_now],
                    arr[9][s_now],
                    arr[10][s_now],
                    arr[11][s_now],
                    arr[12][s_now],
                    arr[13][s_now],
                    arr[14][s_now],
                    arr[15][s_now],
                    arr[slot_obs_end][s_now],
                    *new_act_buf,
                )
            else:
                last_obs = (*base_last, *last_act_buf)
                new_obs = (
                    arr[slot_obs_start][s_now],
                    arr[3][s_now],
                    arr[4][s_now],
                    arr[5][s_now],
                    arr[6][s_now],
                    arr[7][s_now],
                    arr[8][s_now],
                    arr[9][s_now],
                    arr[10][s_now],
                    arr[11][s_now],
                    arr[12][s_now],
                    arr[13][s_now],
                    arr[14][s_now],
                    arr[15][s_now],
                    *new_act_buf,
                )
            new_act = arr[1][s_now]
            rew = np.float32(arr[slot_rewards][s_now])
            terminated = bool(arr[slot_terminated][s_now])
            truncated = bool(arr[slot_truncated][s_now])
            info = arr[slot_info][s_now]
            return last_obs, new_act, rew, new_obs, terminated, truncated, info

        base_last = (
            self.data[slot_obs_start][idx_last],
            self.data[3][idx_last],
            self.data[4][idx_last],
            self.data[5][idx_last],
            self.data[6][idx_last],
            self.data[7][idx_last],
            self.data[8][idx_last],
            self.data[9][idx_last],
            self.data[10][idx_last],
            self.data[11][idx_last],
            self.data[12][idx_last],
            self.data[13][idx_last],
            self.data[14][idx_last],
            self.data[15][idx_last],
        )
        if self.use_curvature:
            last_obs = (*base_last, self.data[16][idx_last], *last_act_buf)
            new_obs = (
                self.data[slot_obs_start][idx_now],
                self.data[3][idx_now],
                self.data[4][idx_now],
                self.data[5][idx_now],
                self.data[6][idx_now],
                self.data[7][idx_now],
                self.data[8][idx_now],
                self.data[9][idx_now],
                self.data[10][idx_now],
                self.data[11][idx_now],
                self.data[12][idx_now],
                self.data[13][idx_now],
                self.data[14][idx_now],
                self.data[15][idx_now],
                self.data[slot_obs_end][idx_now],
                *new_act_buf,
            )
        else:
            last_obs = (*base_last, *last_act_buf)
            new_obs = (
                self.data[slot_obs_start][idx_now],
                self.data[3][idx_now],
                self.data[4][idx_now],
                self.data[5][idx_now],
                self.data[6][idx_now],
                self.data[7][idx_now],
                self.data[8][idx_now],
                self.data[9][idx_now],
                self.data[10][idx_now],
                self.data[11][idx_now],
                self.data[12][idx_now],
                self.data[13][idx_now],
                self.data[14][idx_now],
                self.data[15][idx_now],
                *new_act_buf,
            )

        new_act = self.data[1][idx_now]
        rew = np.float32(self.data[slot_rewards][idx_now])
        terminated = self.data[slot_terminated][idx_now]
        truncated = self.data[slot_truncated][idx_now]
        info = self.data[slot_info][idx_now]
        return last_obs, new_act, rew, new_obs, terminated, truncated, info

    def load_imgs(self, item):
        start = item + self.start_imgs_offset
        end = item + self.start_imgs_offset + self.imgs_obs + 1
        if self._use_contiguous and self._arrays is not None:
            res = [self._arrays[2][self._storage_index_wo(i)] for i in range(start, end)]
            return np.stack(res)
        res = list(islice(self.data[2], start, end))
        return np.stack(res)

    def load_acts(self, item):
        start = item + self.start_acts_offset
        end = item + self.start_acts_offset + self.act_buf_len + 1
        if self._use_contiguous and self._arrays is not None:
            res = [self._arrays[1][self._storage_index_wo(i)] for i in range(start, end)]
            return np.asarray(res)
        res = list(islice(self.data[1], start, end))
        return np.asarray(res)

    def append_buffer(self, buffer):
        """
        buffer is a list of samples ( act, obs, rew, terminated, truncated, info).
        When use_curvature is True, obs must have 15 components (obs[14] = curvature).
        """
        n_new = len(buffer.memory)
        if n_new == 0:
            return self

        if self._use_contiguous:
            # Allocate object-dtype arrays on first append for O(1) random access.
            # Object arrays accept any shape per element (unlike typed arrays that
            # require uniform shapes) while still providing O(1) indexing (unlike deque).
            if self._arrays is None:
                n_slots = 22 if self.use_curvature else 21
                self._arrays = [np.empty((self._max_cap,), dtype=object) for _ in range(n_slots)]
                self.data = [
                    _CircularSlotView(self._arrays[i], self, self._max_cap) for i in range(n_slots)
                ]
            first_data_idx = self._size
            ptr = self._ptr
            max_cap = self._max_cap
            arr = self._arrays
            slot_rewards = 18 if self.use_curvature else 17
            slot_info = 19 if self.use_curvature else 18
            slot_eoes = 17 if self.use_curvature else 16
            slot_term = 20 if self.use_curvature else 19
            slot_trunc = 21 if self.use_curvature else 20
            for i, b in enumerate(buffer.memory):
                pos = (ptr + i) % max_cap
                arr[0][pos] = first_data_idx + i
                arr[1][pos] = b[0]
                for j in range(14):
                    arr[2 + j][pos] = np.array(b[1][j])
                if self.use_curvature:
                    arr[16][pos] = np.array(b[1][14])
                arr[slot_eoes][pos] = b[3] or b[4]
                arr[slot_rewards][pos] = np.float32(b[2])
                arr[slot_info][pos] = b[5]
                arr[slot_term][pos] = b[3]
                arr[slot_trunc][pos] = b[4]
            self._ptr = (ptr + n_new) % max_cap
            self._size = min(self._size + n_new, max_cap)
            self._extend_priorities(n_new)
            to_trim = len(self.priorities) - self._max_cap
            if to_trim > 0:
                self._trim_priorities(to_trim)
            return self

        first_data_idx = self.data[0][-1] + 1 if self.__len__() > 0 else 0

        indices_list = [first_data_idx + i for i, _ in enumerate(buffer.memory)]
        actions_list = [b[0] for b in buffer.memory]
        obs_0_list = [np.array(b[1][0]) for b in buffer.memory]
        obs_1_list = [np.array(b[1][1]) for b in buffer.memory]
        obs_2_list = [np.array(b[1][2]) for b in buffer.memory]
        obs_3_list = [np.array(b[1][3]) for b in buffer.memory]
        obs_4_list = [np.array(b[1][4]) for b in buffer.memory]
        obs_5_list = [np.array(b[1][5]) for b in buffer.memory]
        obs_6_list = [np.array(b[1][6]) for b in buffer.memory]
        obs_7_list = [np.array(b[1][7]) for b in buffer.memory]
        obs_8_list = [np.array(b[1][8]) for b in buffer.memory]
        obs_9_list = [np.array(b[1][9]) for b in buffer.memory]
        obs_10_list = [np.array(b[1][10]) for b in buffer.memory]
        obs_11_list = [np.array(b[1][11]) for b in buffer.memory]
        obs_12_list = [np.array(b[1][12]) for b in buffer.memory]
        obs_13_list = [np.array(b[1][13]) for b in buffer.memory]

        if self.use_curvature:
            # 22 slots: insert curvature at 16, then eoes, rew, info, term, trunc
            curvature_list = [np.array(b[1][14]) for b in buffer.memory]
            eoes_list = [b[3] or b[4] for b in buffer.memory]
            rewards_list = [b[2] for b in buffer.memory]
            infos_list = [b[5] for b in buffer.memory]
            terminated_list = [b[3] for b in buffer.memory]
            truncated_list = [b[4] for b in buffer.memory]
            slot_data_lists = [
                indices_list,
                actions_list,
                obs_0_list,
                obs_1_list,
                obs_2_list,
                obs_3_list,
                obs_4_list,
                obs_5_list,
                obs_6_list,
                obs_7_list,
                obs_8_list,
                obs_9_list,
                obs_10_list,
                obs_11_list,
                obs_12_list,
                obs_13_list,
                curvature_list,
                eoes_list,
                rewards_list,
                infos_list,
                terminated_list,
                truncated_list,
            ]
        else:
            eoes_list = [b[3] or b[4] for b in buffer.memory]
            rewards_list = [b[2] for b in buffer.memory]
            infos_list = [b[5] for b in buffer.memory]
            terminated_list = [b[3] for b in buffer.memory]
            truncated_list = [b[4] for b in buffer.memory]
            slot_data_lists = [
                indices_list,
                actions_list,
                obs_0_list,
                obs_1_list,
                obs_2_list,
                obs_3_list,
                obs_4_list,
                obs_5_list,
                obs_6_list,
                obs_7_list,
                obs_8_list,
                obs_9_list,
                obs_10_list,
                obs_11_list,
                obs_12_list,
                obs_13_list,
                eoes_list,
                rewards_list,
                infos_list,
                terminated_list,
                truncated_list,
            ]

        n_new = len(slot_data_lists[0])
        for slot_idx, slot_data in enumerate(slot_data_lists):
            self.data[slot_idx].extend(slot_data)
        self._extend_priorities(n_new)
        to_trim = len(self.priorities) - self.memory_size
        if to_trim > 0:
            self._trim_priorities(to_trim)

        return self

    def sample(self):
        n_obs = 15 if self.use_curvature else 14
        return _sample_with_hflip(self, n_obs, has_curvature=self.use_curvature, has_images=False)


# ============================ R2D2 MEMORY TQC with Images ==================


class MemoryR2D2TQCWithImages(R2D2Memory):
    def __init__(
        self,
        memory_size=None,
        batch_size=None,
        dataset_path="",
        imgs_obs=4,
        act_buf_len=1,
        nb_steps=2,
        sample_preprocessor: Callable[..., Any] | None = None,
        crc_debug=False,
        device="cpu",
        use_curvature=False,
    ):
        self.imgs_obs = imgs_obs
        self.act_buf_len = act_buf_len
        self.use_curvature = bool(use_curvature)
        self.n_obs = 16 if self.use_curvature else 15
        self.min_samples = max(self.imgs_obs, self.act_buf_len)
        self.start_imgs_offset = max(0, self.min_samples - self.imgs_obs)
        self.start_acts_offset = max(0, self.min_samples - self.act_buf_len)

        self._use_contiguous = True
        self._ptr = 0
        self._size = 0
        self._max_cap = int(memory_size or 1000000)
        self._arrays = None

        super().__init__(
            memory_size=memory_size,
            batch_size=batch_size,
            dataset_path=dataset_path,
            nb_steps=nb_steps,
            sample_preprocessor=sample_preprocessor,
            crc_debug=crc_debug,
            device=device,
        )
        self._max_cap = int(self.memory_size)
        self._eoes_idx = 2 + self.n_obs
        self._rew_idx = 3 + self.n_obs
        self._info_idx = 4 + self.n_obs
        self._term_idx = 5 + self.n_obs
        self._trunc_idx = 6 + self.n_obs
        self._n_slots = 7 + self.n_obs
        self.rewards_index = self._rew_idx
        if len(self.data) == 0:
            self.data = []
        else:
            self._use_contiguous = False
            self.data = [deque(slot_deque, maxlen=self.memory_size) for slot_deque in self.data]

    def __len__(self):
        if self._use_contiguous:
            if self._arrays is None or self._size == 0:
                return 0
            return max(0, self._size - self.min_samples - 1)
        if len(self.data) == 0:
            return 0
        return max(0, len(self.data[0]) - self.min_samples - 1)

    def _storage_index(self, step_idx: int) -> int:
        if step_idx < 0 or step_idx >= self._size:
            raise IndexError(f"step_idx {step_idx} out of range [0, {self._size})")
        return (self._ptr - self._size + step_idx) % self._max_cap

    def get_transition(self, item):
        idx_last = item + self.min_samples - 1
        idx_now = item + self.min_samples
        acts = self.load_acts(item)
        last_act_buf = acts[:-1]
        new_act_buf = acts[1:]
        slot_obs_start = 2

        if self._use_contiguous and self._arrays is not None:
            s_last = self._storage_index(idx_last)
            s_now = self._storage_index(idx_now)
            arr = self._arrays
            base_last = tuple(arr[slot_obs_start + i][s_last] for i in range(self.n_obs))
            base_now = tuple(arr[slot_obs_start + i][s_now] for i in range(self.n_obs))
            last_obs = (*base_last, *last_act_buf)
            new_obs = (*base_now, *new_act_buf)
            new_act = arr[1][s_now]
            rew = np.float32(arr[self._rew_idx][s_now])
            terminated = bool(arr[self._term_idx][s_now])
            truncated = bool(arr[self._trunc_idx][s_now])
            info = arr[self._info_idx][s_now]
            return last_obs, new_act, rew, new_obs, terminated, truncated, info

        base_last = tuple(self.data[slot_obs_start + i][idx_last] for i in range(self.n_obs))
        base_now = tuple(self.data[slot_obs_start + i][idx_now] for i in range(self.n_obs))
        last_obs = (*base_last, *last_act_buf)
        new_obs = (*base_now, *new_act_buf)
        new_act = self.data[1][idx_now]
        rew = np.float32(self.data[self._rew_idx][idx_now])
        terminated = self.data[self._term_idx][idx_now]
        truncated = self.data[self._trunc_idx][idx_now]
        info = self.data[self._info_idx][idx_now]
        return last_obs, new_act, rew, new_obs, terminated, truncated, info

    def load_imgs(self, item):
        img_slot = 2 + self.n_obs - 1
        start = item + self.start_imgs_offset
        end = item + self.start_imgs_offset + self.imgs_obs + 1
        if self._use_contiguous and self._arrays is not None:
            res = [self._arrays[img_slot][self._storage_index(i)] for i in range(start, end)]
            return np.stack(res).astype(np.float32) / 256.0
        res = [self.data[img_slot][j] for j in range(start, end)]
        return np.stack(res).astype(np.float32) / 256.0

    def load_acts(self, item):
        start = item + self.start_acts_offset
        end = item + self.start_acts_offset + self.act_buf_len + 1
        if self._use_contiguous and self._arrays is not None:
            return [self._arrays[1][self._storage_index(i)] for i in range(start, end)]
        return [self.data[1][j] for j in range(start, end)]

    def append_buffer(self, buffer):
        n_new = len(buffer.memory)
        if n_new == 0:
            return self

        if self._use_contiguous:
            if self._arrays is None:
                n_slots = self._n_slots
                self._arrays = [np.empty((self._max_cap,), dtype=object) for _ in range(n_slots)]
                self.data = [
                    _CircularSlotView(self._arrays[i], self, self._max_cap) for i in range(n_slots)
                ]
            first_data_idx = self._size
            ptr = self._ptr
            max_cap = self._max_cap
            arr = self._arrays
            n_obs_components = self.n_obs
            for i, b in enumerate(buffer.memory):
                pos = (ptr + i) % max_cap
                arr[0][pos] = first_data_idx + i
                arr[1][pos] = b[0]
                for j in range(n_obs_components):
                    arr[2 + j][pos] = np.array(b[1][j])
                arr[self._eoes_idx][pos] = b[3] or b[4]
                arr[self._rew_idx][pos] = np.float32(b[2])
                arr[self._info_idx][pos] = b[5]
                arr[self._term_idx][pos] = b[3]
                arr[self._trunc_idx][pos] = b[4]
            self._ptr = (ptr + n_new) % max_cap
            self._size = min(self._size + n_new, max_cap)
            self._extend_priorities(n_new)
            to_trim = len(self.priorities) - self._max_cap
            if to_trim > 0:
                self._trim_priorities(to_trim)
            return self

        first_data_idx = self.data[0][-1] + 1 if len(self.data) > 0 and len(self.data[0]) > 0 else 0
        n_obs_components = self.n_obs
        slot_data_lists = [
            [first_data_idx + i for i, _ in enumerate(buffer.memory)],
            [b[0] for b in buffer.memory],
        ]
        for i in range(n_obs_components):
            slot_data_lists.append([np.array(b[1][i]) for b in buffer.memory])
        slot_data_lists.append([b[3] or b[4] for b in buffer.memory])
        slot_data_lists.append([b[2] for b in buffer.memory])
        slot_data_lists.append([b[5] for b in buffer.memory])
        slot_data_lists.append([b[3] for b in buffer.memory])
        slot_data_lists.append([b[4] for b in buffer.memory])
        n_new_d = len(slot_data_lists[0])
        for slot_idx, slot_data in enumerate(slot_data_lists):
            self.data[slot_idx].extend(slot_data)
        self._extend_priorities(n_new_d)
        to_trim = len(self.priorities) - self.memory_size
        if to_trim > 0:
            self._trim_priorities(to_trim)
        return self

    def sample(self):
        return _sample_with_hflip(
            self, self.n_obs, has_curvature=self.use_curvature, has_images=True
        )


# ============================ R2D2 MEMORY without Images ==================


class MemoryR2D2Sophy(R2D2Memory):
    def __init__(
        self,
        memory_size=None,
        batch_size=None,
        dataset_path="",
        imgs_obs=4,
        act_buf_len=1,
        nb_steps=2,
        sample_preprocessor: Callable[..., Any] | None = None,
        crc_debug=False,
        device="cpu",
    ):
        self.imgs_obs = imgs_obs
        self.act_buf_len = act_buf_len
        self.min_samples = max(self.imgs_obs, self.act_buf_len)
        self.start_imgs_offset = max(0, self.min_samples - self.imgs_obs)
        self.start_acts_offset = max(0, self.min_samples - self.act_buf_len)

        super().__init__(
            memory_size=memory_size,
            batch_size=batch_size,
            dataset_path=dataset_path,
            nb_steps=nb_steps,
            sample_preprocessor=sample_preprocessor,
            crc_debug=crc_debug,
            device=device,
        )
        n_slots = 21
        if len(self.data) == 0:
            self.data = [deque(maxlen=self.memory_size) for _ in range(n_slots)]
        else:
            self.data = [deque(slot_deque, maxlen=self.memory_size) for slot_deque in self.data]

    def get_transition(self, item):
        """
        CAUTION: item is the first index of the 4 images in the OLD observation's image history.
        CAUTION: in the buffer, a sample is (act, obs(act)) and NOT (obs, act(obs))
            i.e. the observation is what step returned after being fed act (and preprocessed);
            in RTRL, act is appended to obs.
        So we load 5 images from here...
        Don't forget the info dict for CRC debugging
        """
        idx_last = item + self.min_samples - 1
        idx_now = item + self.min_samples

        acts = self.load_acts(item)
        last_act_buf = acts[:-1]
        new_act_buf = acts[1:]

        slot_actions = 1
        slot_obs_start = 2
        slot_obs_end = 16
        slot_rewards = 17
        slot_infos = 18
        slot_terminated = 19
        slot_truncated = 20

        last_obs = (
            self.data[slot_obs_start][idx_last],
            self.data[3][idx_last],
            self.data[4][idx_last],
            self.data[5][idx_last],
            self.data[6][idx_last],
            self.data[7][idx_last],
            self.data[8][idx_last],
            self.data[9][idx_last],
            self.data[10][idx_last],
            self.data[11][idx_last],
            self.data[12][idx_last],
            self.data[13][idx_last],
            self.data[14][idx_last],
            self.data[slot_obs_end][idx_last],
            *last_act_buf,
        )
        new_act = self.data[slot_actions][idx_now]
        rew = np.float32(self.data[slot_rewards][idx_now])
        new_obs = (
            self.data[slot_obs_start][idx_now],
            self.data[3][idx_now],
            self.data[4][idx_now],
            self.data[5][idx_now],
            self.data[6][idx_now],
            self.data[7][idx_now],
            self.data[8][idx_now],
            self.data[9][idx_now],
            self.data[10][idx_now],
            self.data[11][idx_now],
            self.data[12][idx_now],
            self.data[13][idx_now],
            self.data[14][idx_now],
            self.data[slot_obs_end][idx_now],
            *new_act_buf,
        )
        terminated = self.data[slot_terminated][idx_now]
        truncated = self.data[slot_truncated][idx_now]
        info = self.data[slot_infos][idx_now]
        return last_obs, new_act, rew, new_obs, terminated, truncated, info

    def load_imgs(self, item):
        res = self.data[2][
            (item + self.start_imgs_offset) : (item + self.start_imgs_offset + self.imgs_obs + 1)
        ]
        return np.stack(res)

    def load_acts(self, item):
        res = self.data[1][
            (item + self.start_acts_offset) : (item + self.start_acts_offset + self.act_buf_len + 1)
        ]
        return res

    def append_buffer(self, buffer):
        # line 532, in get_transition
        # self.data[23][idx_last],  # 21
        # IndexError: list index out of range
        """
        buffer is a list of samples ( act, obs, rew, terminated, truncated, info)
        don't forget to keep the info dictionary in the sample for CRC debugging
        """

        first_data_idx = self.data[0][-1] + 1 if self.__len__() > 0 else 0

        indices_list = [first_data_idx + i for i, _ in enumerate(buffer.memory)]
        actions_list = [b[0] for b in buffer.memory]
        track_list = [np.array(b[1][0]) for b in buffer.memory]
        speeds_list = [np.array(b[1][1]) for b in buffer.memory]
        accelerations_list = [np.array(b[1][2]) for b in buffer.memory]
        jerks_list = [np.array(b[1][3]) for b in buffer.memory]
        race_progress_list = [np.array(b[1][4]) for b in buffer.memory]
        input_steer_list = [np.array(b[1][5]) for b in buffer.memory]
        input_gas_pedal_list = [np.array(b[1][6]) for b in buffer.memory]
        input_brake_list = [np.array(b[1][7]) for b in buffer.memory]
        gear_list = [np.array(b[1][8]) for b in buffer.memory]
        aim_yaw_list = [np.array(b[1][9]) for b in buffer.memory]
        aim_pitch_list = [np.array(b[1][10]) for b in buffer.memory]
        steer_angle_list = [np.array(b[1][11]) for b in buffer.memory]
        slip_coef_list = [np.array(b[1][12]) for b in buffer.memory]
        failure_counter_list = [np.array(b[1][13]) for b in buffer.memory]
        eoes_list = [b[3] or b[4] for b in buffer.memory]
        rewards_list = [b[2] for b in buffer.memory]
        infos_list = [b[5] for b in buffer.memory]
        terminated_list = [b[3] for b in buffer.memory]
        truncated_list = [b[4] for b in buffer.memory]
        n_new = len(indices_list)
        slot_data_lists = [
            indices_list,
            actions_list,
            track_list,
            speeds_list,
            accelerations_list,
            jerks_list,
            race_progress_list,
            input_steer_list,
            input_gas_pedal_list,
            input_brake_list,
            gear_list,
            aim_yaw_list,
